**You must only answer ONE PAIR.**

**Step 2:** **Check if any spatial propositions** from the list above apply to this contradiction

**Step 3:** **If applicable propositions exist,** explicitly state which proposition(s) and explain how they reveal the contradiction

**Step 5:** **Provide your answer in JSON format**

**The answer JSON should strictly be the last line, followed by nothing else.**

LLM OUTPUT (RAW RESPONSE):
------------------------------
**Step 1:** Analyze the spatial contradiction between evidences and testimonies. 
From the testimonies, Ini mentions walking her sister to the Channeling Chamber (Testimony 3) and not seeing anyone on the way (Testimony 4). However, Evidence 12 and Evidence 13 show Lotta taking pictures of Maya at the crime scene, indicating Lotta was present.

**Step 2:** Check if any spatial propositions from the list above apply to this contradiction. 
Proposition 6 states that if Ini is walking to the Channeling Chamber, then Ini is in the hallway. This implies that Ini should have seen Lotta if she was indeed in the hallway or Channeling Chamber, given the context of Lotta's photos being taken after the gunshot.

**Step 3:** If applicable propositions exist, explicitly state which proposition(s) and explain how they reveal the contradiction. 
Proposition 6 is relevant because it establishes Ini's path to the Channeling Chamber, which would involve the hallway. Given that Lotta was in the Channeling Chamber (as evidenced by her photos), Ini's claim of not seeing anyone contradicts the expected presence of Lotta, especially considering the spatial layout and Ini's path.

**Step 4:** If none of the listed propositions apply, explain the spatial reasoning used to identify the contradiction. 
While the direct application of Proposition 6 highlights a potential discrepancy, the core contradiction lies in the testimonies about seeing or not seeing individuals during the events leading up to the murder. Ini's testimony about not seeing anyone (Testimony 4) directly contradicts the evidence of Lotta's presence (Evidences 12 and 13).

**Step 5:** Provide your answer in JSON format. 
Given the analysis, the contradiction arises from Ini's testimony of not seeing anyone on the way to the Channeling Chamber and the evidence showing Lotta's presence. Therefore, the contradiction is between Testimony 4 and Evidence 12 (or 13, but we'll focus on one for the format requirement).
